## doc_4e4afb29c8afe53f:scheme4_proposed_mechanism

类型：mechanism；状态：needs_review；完整性：partial


备注：Authors propose dehydration to carbenium ion A, resonance form B, water attack to enol C, isomerization to enone D, phospha-Michael addition to E, and enol-keto tautomerism to 3.

备注：Intermediates A-E are proposed, not reported as isolated compounds.
